# Selector ablation runs (ablation.tex: tab:alpha-ablation,
# tab:lambda-ablation, tab:ensemble-size-ablation).
#
# SAPA / dog-bird, 5 targets x 4 victims = 20 trials per run, targets pinned to
# the first 5 of target_sets/<model>_gradmatch_dog-bird.json in file order.
#
# Everything here varies one selector knob off the default (lambda=1, alpha=2,
# K=20): Greedy is --base ours with no --sel_* flag, alpha lives in the _seldpp<a>
# tag, lambda in _lam<l>, and the selector ensemble size in _K<n>.
#
# Deliberately NOT in dpp_done.txt: that file feeds table.tex, whose cells are
# 10 x 6 sweep runs. These are 5 x 4 and would be double counted there.
